## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-13T02:41:49.543Z

**📎 ToolResult**

```
using System;
using Microsoft.Xna.Framework;

namespace Terraria;

public abstract class Entity : IEntitySourceTarget
{
	public int whoAmI;

	public Vector2 position;

	public Vector2 velocity;

	public Vector2 oldPosition;

	public Vector2 oldVelocity;

	public int oldDirection;

	public int direction = 1;

	public int width;

	public int height;

	public bool wet;

	public bool shimmerWet;

	public bool honeyWet;

	public byte wetCount;

	public bool lavaWet;

	public bool AnyWet
	{
		get
		{
			if (!wet && !lavaWet && !honeyWet)
			{
				return shimmerWet;
			}
			return true;
		}
	}

	public virtual Vector2 VisualPosition => position;

	public Vector2 Center
	{
		get
		{
			return new Vector2(position.X + (float)width / 2f, position.Y + (float)height / 2f);
		}
		set
		{
			position = new Vector2(value.X - (float)width / 2f, value.Y - (float)height / 2f);
		}
	}

	public Vector2 Left
	{
		get
		{
			return new Vector2(position.X, position.Y + (float)height / 2f);
		}
		set
		{
			position = new Vector2(value.X, value.Y - (float)height / 2f);
		}
	}

	public Vector2 Right
	{
		get
		{
			return new Vector2(position.X + (float)width, position.Y + (float)height / 2f);
		}
		set
		{
			position = new Vector2(value.X - (float)width, value.Y - (float)height / 2f);
		}
	}

	public Vector2 Top
	{
		get
		{
			return new Vector2(position.X + (float)width / 2f, position.Y);
		}
		set
		{
			position = new Vector2(value.X - (float)width / 2f, value.Y);
		}
	}

	public Vector2 TopLeft
	{
		get
		{
			return position;
		}
		set
		{
			position = value;
		}
	}

	public Vector2 TopRight
	{
		get
		{
			return new Vector2(position.X + (float)width, position.Y);
		}
		set
		{
			position = new Vector2(value.X - (float)width, value.Y);
		}
	}

	public Vector2 Bottom
	{
		get
		{
			return new Vector2(position.X + (float)width / 2f, position.Y + (float)height);
		}
		set
		{
			position = new Vector2(value.X - (float)width / 2f, value.Y - (float)height);
		}
	}

	public Vector2 BottomLeft
	{
		get
		{
			return new Vector2(position.X, position.Y + (float)height);
		}
		set
		{
			position = new Vector2(value.X, value.Y - (float)height);
		}
	}

	public Vector2 BottomRight
	{
		get
		{
			return new Vector2(position.X + (float)width, position.Y + (float)height);
		}
		set
		{
			position = new Vector2(value.X - (float)width, value.Y - (float)height);
		}
	}

	public Vector2 Size
	{
		get
		{
			return new Vector2(width, height);
		}
		set
		{
			width = (int)value.X;
			height = (int)value.Y;
		}
	}

	public Rectangle Hitbox
	{
		get
		{
			return new Rectangle((int)position.X, (int)position.Y, width, height);
		}
		set
		{
			position = new Vector2(value.X, value.Y);
			width = value.Width;
			height = value.Height;
		}
	}

	public float AngleTo(Vector2 Destination)
	{
		return (float)Math.Atan2(Destination.Y - Center.Y, Destination.X - Center.X);
	}

	public float AngleFrom(Vector2 Source)
	{
		return (float)Math.Atan2(Center.Y - Source.Y, Center.X - Source.X);
	}

	public float Distance(Vector2 Other)
	{
		return Vector2.Distance(Center, Other);
	}

	public float DistanceSQ(Vector2 Other)
	{
		return Vector2.DistanceSquared(Center, Other);
	}

	public Vector2 DirectionTo(Vector2 Destination)
	{
		return Vector2.Normalize(Destination - Center);
	}

	public Vector2 DirectionFrom(Vector2 Source)
	{
		return Vector2.Normalize(Center - Source);
	}

	public bool WithinRange(Vector2 Target, float MaxRange)
	{
		return Vector2.DistanceSquared(Center, Target) <= MaxRange * MaxRange;
	}
}

	private void TryRemovingWaterPerishableEffects(bool isInLava)
	{

		{
			for (int i = 0; i < maxBuffs; i++)
			{
				int num = buffType[i];
				if (num == 24 || num == 323)
				{
					DelBuff(i);
				}
			}
		}
		if (!stinky || Main.netMode == 1)
		{
			return;
		}
		for (int j = 0; j < maxBuffs; j++)
		{
			if (buffType[j] == 120)

	private void TryAddingRepeatedBuff(int buffId, int timeToGive, int lowThresholdUndeathToAllowBuff = 10)
	{
		if (!buffImmune[buffId])

			if (num == -1 || buffTime[num] <= lowThresholdUndeathToAllowBuff)
			{
				AddBuff(buffId, timeToGive);
			}
		}
	}

					}
				}
			}
		}
		return flag;
	}

		position.Y = vector.Y;
		velocity.X = vector.Z;
		velocity.Y = vector.W;
	}

Excellent. This is the core. Now key facts so far:

**Wet detection chain (UpdateCollision, NPC.cs:93712):**
1. `Collision_WalkDownSlopes()`
2. `lava = Collision_LavaCollision()` — LavaCollision; if lava → lavaWet=true; if !lavaImmune → AddBuff(24=OnFire, 420) + StrikeNPCNoInteraction(50) every 30 frames (immune[255]).
3. `lava = Collision_WaterCollision(lava)`:
   - Water-immune-to-wet list: `(type==690 && ai[0]==0) || type==72 || aiStyle==21 || aiStyle==67 || type==376 || type==579 || type==541 || (aiStyle==7 && ai[0]==25)` → flag=false, wetCount=0, lava=false. (these never get "wet")
   - else flag = Collision.WetCollision(position,width,height); honey→honeyWet; shimmer→shimmerWet (+buff 353).
   - if flag && wetCount==0: splash dust/sound on entering; wet=true.
   - else if wet && !flag: velocity.X *= 0.5f; wet=false; exit splash.
4. `if (!wet) { lavaWet=false; honeyWet=false; shimmerWet=false; }`
5. wetCount-- if >0.
6. oldVelocity = velocity; `oldDryVelocity = velocity`; ApplyTileCollision(...); then if wet → Collision_MoveWhileWet(oldDryVelocity, speed) where speed = shimmerMovementSpeed/honeyMovementSpeed(0.25)/lavaMovementSpeed(0.5)/waterMovementSpeed(0.5).

**Collision_MoveWhileWet(oldDryVelocity, Slowdown):**
```
if (Collision.up) velocity.Y = 0.01f;
vector = velocity * Slowdown;
if (velocity.X != oldDryVelocity.X) { vector.X = velocity.X; collideX = true; }
if (velocity.Y != oldDryVelocity.Y) { vector.Y = velocity.Y; collideY = true; }
oldPosition = position; oldDirection = direction; position += vector;
```
Note: `position += velocity * Slowdown` — the position is moved by scaled velocity, but velocity itself is NOT modified. Important semantic: the NPC's velocity field stays; only the actual positional movement is halved. And if tile collision changed velocity (velocity != oldDryVelocity), use the actual velocity (so no double-slowing on collision axes).

**Speeds default:** water 0.5, lava 0.5, honey 0.25. Set to 1f for: DD2 (LazySetLiquidMovementDD2), critters type 613 (scorpion etc? Actually 612-613 are critters... line 16810 region — type 613 area sets waterMovementSpeed = 1f; let me check what type range that is).

## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-13T02:42:29.555Z

```
// 飞行避水通用行为：前方探测液体 → 上抛反弹/爬升。
// 任何飞行实体（怪物/小动物）按需套用：def.avoidWater = true。
import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
import type { World } from '../world/World';

export interface AvoidBody {
  x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number;
  vx: number; vy: number;
}

/**
 * @param flipX true=水平反弹（蝙蝠式弹开），false=保持航向只爬升（鸭子式拉起）
 * @returns 是否发生了避让（调用方通常本帧不再移动）
 */
export function avoidWater(b: AvoidBody, world: World, flipX = true): boolean {
  const ax = Math.floor((b.x + b.w / 2 + b.vx * 4) / TILE);
  const ay = Math.floor((b.y + b.h / 2 + b.vy * 4) / TILE);
  if (!world.store.inBounds(ax, ay)) return false;
  if (world.store.liquid[world.store.idx(ax, ay)] <= 40) return false;
  // 前方是液体：上抛脱离
  b.vy = -Math.abs(b.vy || 1) * 0.8 - 0.8;
  if (flipX) b.vx = -b.vx * 0.5;
  else b.vx *= 0.7; // 保留航向，只爬升越过水面
  return true;
}
// 实体基类与分桶管理器
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import type { Camera } from '../render/Camera';
import type { Renderer } from '../render/Renderer';

export abstract class Entity {
  id = 0;
  x = 0; y = 0;          // 左上角 px
  w = 16; h = 16;
  vx = 0; vy = 0;
  dead = false;
  onGround = false;
  hitWall = false;
  hitHead = false;
  dropThrough = false;
  // ---- 联机傀儡标记（v3 实体同步）：快照驱动实体，本地不跑 AI/命中判定 ----
  netPuppet = false;
  /** 联机实体号（拥有者 slot 高 8 位 + 本地 id 低 24 位；见 net/entitySyncHooks） */
  netId = 0;
  // ---- P3.2 傀儡插值缓冲（15Hz 快照 × 60TPS 渲染的平滑桥）----
  /** 最新权威快照位（NPC 接触判定用；弹幕侧判定用渲染位——见 Game 两处接触注释） */
  netAx = 0; netAy = 0;
  /** 插值起点（快照到达时的渲染位）与进度（1=已到目标，纯外推） */
  netIx = 0; netIy = 0; netIt = 1;
  private netSnapIvt = 4;

  abstract fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks): void;
  abstract draw(r: Renderer, cam: Camera): void;

  /** 快照到达（Game.netApplyNpcSnaps / netApplyProjSnaps 调用）：设插值目标。
   *  从当前渲染位连续出发（中途到包不跳变）；intervalTicks = 快照周期（15Hz=4 tick） */
  netSnapTo(x: number, y: number, intervalTicks = 4) {
    this.netIx = this.x; this.netIy = this.y;
    this.netAx = x; this.netAy = y;
    this.netIt = 0;
    this.netSnapIvt = Math.max(1, intervalTicks);
  }

  /** 傀儡每 tick 推进：未到目标走 smoothstep 插值（一个快照周期到达），到后纯速度外推 */
  netPuppetStep() {
    if (this.netIt < 1) {
      this.netIt = Math.min(1, this.netIt + 1 / this.netSnapIvt);
      const t = this.netIt * this.netIt * (3 - 2 * this.netIt); // smoothstep
      this.x = this.netIx + (this.netAx - this.netIx) * t;
      this.y = this.netIy + (this.netAy - this.netIy) * t;
      return;
    }
    this.x += this.vx;
    this.y += this.vy;
  }

  get cx() { return this.x + this.w / 2; }
  get cy() { return this.y + this.h / 2; }
  aabbOverlaps(o: Entity): boolean {
    return this.x < o.x + o.w && this.x + this.w > o.x && this.y < o.y + o.h && this.y + this.h > o.y;
  }
}

/** 就地剔除 dead 实体(保持顺序,零分配) */
function compact(list: Entity[]): void {
  let w = 0;
  for (let r = 0; r < list.length; r++) {
    const e = list[r];
    if (!e.dead) list[w++] = e;
  }
  list.length = w;
}

export class EntityManager {
  nextId = 1;
  enemies: Entity[] = [];
  drops: Entity[] = [];
  npcs: Entity[] = [];
  projectiles: Entity[] = [];
  props: Entity[] = [];   // 场景物件（墓碑等不可拾取物理实体）
  critters: Entity[] = []; // 小动物（无害被动生物）

  add(e: Entity, bucket: 'enemies' | 'drops' | 'npcs' | 'projectiles' | 'props' | 'critters') {
    e.id = this.nextId++;
    this[bucket].push(e);
  }

  update(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
    // 就地压缩替代 6×filter(2026-08 审计 G9:每 tick 6 个新数组,60Hz 恒定分配)
    for (const list of [this.enemies, this.drops, this.npcs, this.projectiles, this.props, this.critters]) {
      for (const e of list) e.fixedUpdate(dt, game);
      compact(list);
    }
  }

  clear() {
    this.enemies.length = 0;
    this.drops.length = 0;
    this.npcs.length = 0;
    this.projectiles.length = 0;
    this.props.length = 0;
    this.critters.length = 0;
  }

  all(): Entity[] {
    return [...this.enemies, ...this.drops, ...this.npcs, ...this.projectiles, ...this.props, ...this.critters];
  }
}

```

682	  constructor(public key: string, x: number, y: number) {
683	    super();
684	    this.def = ENEMY_DEFS[key] ?? PLACEHOLDER_DEF;
685	    this.hp = this.def.hp;
686	    this.maxHp = this.def.hp;
687	    this.w = this.def.width;
688	    this.h = this.def.height;
689	    this.x = x - this.w / 2;
690	    this.y = y - this.h / 2;
691	  }
692	
693	  fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
694	    this.prevX = this.x; this.prevY = this.y;
695	    // 联机傀儡（房主权威快照驱动）：无本地 AI——P3.2 插值/外推推进 + 动画计时，
696	    // 打击/拾取等交互经 entityNet 上报房主结算
697	    if (this.netPuppet) {
698	      this.aiT++;
699	      this.animT++;
700	      this.netPuppetStep();
701	      this.onGround = Math.abs(this.vy) < 0.6;
702	      if (Math.abs(this.vx) > 0.05) this.walkCycleT += Math.abs(this.vx);
703	      if (this.iframes > 0) this.iframes--;
704	      this.pushHistory();   // 傀儡无本地 AI 但拖尾渲染同样需要历史位置
705	      return;
706	    }
707	    this.aiT++;
708	    if (this.iframes > 0) this.iframes--;
709	    if (this.justHitT > 0) this.justHitT--;
710	    this.lightRGB = null; // 点光由 AI 每帧重写（不残留上一帧状态）
711	    this.lightRGBAt = null;
712	    if (this.hpBarT > 0) this.hpBarT--;
713	    if (this.whipTagT > 0) this.whipTagT--;
714	    if (this.whipProcT > 0) this.whipProcT--;
715	    if (this.squash !== 0) this.squash *= 0.85;
716	    this.animT++;
717	    this.walkCycleT += Math.abs(this.vx); // 原版 zombie/fighter 行走帧按 |vx| 累加（L77072/L70216）
718	
719	    // EoW 族出生渐显（每 tick alpha-1;原版 AI_006）——静态半透明不受影响
720	    if (this.alphaFade) {
721	      this.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, this.spawnAlpha - 1);
722	      if (this.spawnAlpha === 0) this.alphaFade = false;
723	    }
724	
725	    const player = (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player;
726	    const hasPlayer = !!player && !player.dead;
727	
728	    // ---- 原版数据驱动路径：按 aiStyle 分发到已移植的 AI 家族（分发后落入共享尾段） ----
729	    // 蠕虫身体段（wormFollow 非空）无 AI：位置由头部 wormAI 沿链驱动，但仍走共享尾段（接触伤害等）
730	    // 例外：EoW 三段各段独立跑 eowAI（断链自查 + 身/尾自跟随，:51874/:52266），
731	    // 不再由头统一驱动链——断链拆分后每条子虫的头各自驱动
732	    const eowSeg = this.vanillaId !== null && this.vanillaId >= 13 && this.vanillaId <= 15;
733	    const wormBody = this.wormFollow && !eowSeg;
734	    // 小动物（critter）优先走被动游荡，绝不落入 default 的追击 fallback
735	    if (this.vanilla && !wormBody && this.vanilla.critter) {
736	      this.critterWanderAI(game, hasPlayer ? player : null);
737	    } else if (this.vanilla && !wormBody) {
738	      const p = hasPlayer ? player : null;
739	      switch (this.vanilla.aiStyle) {
740	        case 92: this.dummyAI(game); break;                            // AI_092 训练假人（NPC 488）
741	        case 1: this.slimeAI(game, p); break;                          // AI_001 史莱姆
742	        case 2: this.floatEyeAI(game, p); break;                        // AI_002 飘浮眼（原版核）
743	        case 3:
744	          // AI_003 拜月教弓手 379/380 仪式待机（NPC.cs:56575-56601,ai[3]<0 静立面碑零伤；
745	          // 碑置 ai[3]=0 后激活复位 :56602-56609,回战士核）——bossAI_lunar_misc
746	          if ((this.vanillaId === 379 || this.vanillaId === 380) && this.ai3 < 0) {
747	            cultistArcherStandbyAI(this, game);
748	            break;
749	          }
750	          if (this.vanillaId === 379 || this.vanillaId === 380) cultistArcherWake(this);
751	          this.fighterAI(game, p);
752	          // 火把僵尸 590/591 手持火把发光（NPC.cs:57309-57366 AI_003 内 type 段）：
753	          //  590 按朝向取 (Center±22 / y+6) 格，火把格与相邻格均无液体才点亮；
754	          //  591 持械变体 !wet 即亮，偏移 ±36/±28（专家变体，尘需 ai[2]==0 门）。
755	          //  色 (1, 0.95, 0.8) = 与手持火把同色；尘 6 号 1/30 概率
756	          if (this.vanillaId === 590 || this.vanillaId === 591) this.torchZombieLight(game);

758	          if (this.vanillaId === 383 || this.vanillaId === 386) martianFighterExtras(this, game, p);

761	        // 冲刺计时合法冻结;传 null 会让 aiT 照走、状态机停摆 → 首冲时长翻倍)

767	        case 11:

// 分轴扫描 tile AABB 碰撞
import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
import type { World } from '../world/World';

export interface Body {
  x: number; y: number;       // 左上角（px）
  w: number; h: number;
  vx: number; vy: number;
  onGround: boolean;
  hitWall: boolean;
  hitHead: boolean;
  /** 平台单向碰撞：仅当开启且下落时与平台碰撞 */
  dropThrough?: boolean;
  /** 启用原版自动上台阶（Collision.StepUp）：行走实体（玩家/敌人/小动物/城镇NPC）置 true；
   *  掉落物/弹幕/墓碑等不参与（原版它们不调 StepUp） */
  stepUp?: boolean;
}

/** Collision.StepUp（Collision.cs:3641-3770，gravDir=1、holdsMatching=false）：
 *  水平行走【意图速度】先探前方一列 → 满足净空门即抬升最多 16.1px
 *  （半砖 8px / 整砖台阶 16px）。玩家（Player.cs:23258/:27753）与全部 NPC
 *  （NPC.cs:54382，先于 TileCollision 用未受阻的速度调用）共用——
 *  此前只有玩家有自研版上台阶，敌人/小动物/城镇 NPC 全部卡死在半砖/台阶。
 *  gfxOffY 视觉缓动无对应渲染通道，位置直接抬升（原版 NPC 同样瞬间抬）。 */
function applyStepUp(b: Body, world: World): void {
  const vx = b.vx;
  if (vx === 0) return;
  const dir = Math.sign(vx);
  const st = world.store;
  const probeX = b.x + vx;                       // vector.X = position.X + velocity.X
  const num2 = Math.floor((probeX + b.w / 2 + (b.w / 2 + 1) * dir) / TILE);
  const num3 = Math.floor((b.y + b.h - 1) / TILE); // 脚底行（gravDir=1）
  const num4 = Math.floor(b.h / TILE) + (b.h % TILE !== 0 ? 1 : 0);
  if (!st.inBounds(num2, num3) || num3 >= st.h - 40) return;
  const solidAt = (x: number, y: number): boolean =>
    st.inBounds(x, y) && st.isSolid(x, y);       // 平台非 tileSolid → 不阻挡（vanilla 语义）
  const halfAt = (x: number, y: number): boolean =>
    st.inBounds(x, y) && !!st.half[st.idx(x, y)];
  const slopeAt = (x: number, y: number): number =>
    st.inBounds(x, y) ? st.slope[st.idx(x, y)] : 0;
  // flag（:3700-3708）：身体放入探柱（j=2..num4 上方行全净空）
  for (let j = 2; j <= num4; j++) if (solidAt(num2, num3 - j)) return;
  // flag2（:3710-3714）：后上方对角净空
  if (solidAt(num2 - dir, num3 - num4)) return;
  const centerX = b.x + b.w / 2;
  // flag3（:3725-3728）：脚上一格净空 / 面朝坡 / 半砖且其上净空
  {
    const s = slopeAt(num2, num3 - 1);
    const ok = !solidAt(num2, num3 - 1)
      || (s === 1 && centerX > num2 * TILE)
      || (s === 2 && centerX < num2 * TILE + TILE)
      || (halfAt(num2, num3 - 1) && !solidAt(num2, num3 - num4 - 1));
    if (!ok) return;
  }
  // flag4（:3730-3737）：落脚格实心（面朝坡且身体沉入）/ 或脚上一格是半砖
  {
    const fs = slopeAt(num2, num3);
    const topSlope = fs === 1 || fs === 2;
    const a = solidAt(num2, num3)
      && (!topSlope || (fs === 1 && centerX < num2 * TILE) || (fs === 2 && centerX > num2 * TILE + TILE))
      && (!topSlope || b.y + b.h > num3 * TILE);
    const bb = halfAt(num2, num3 - 1) && solidAt(num2, num3 - 1);
    if (!(a || bb)) return;
  }
  // X 重叠门（:3745-3748）：探柱与移动后身体横向相交
  if (!(num2 * TILE < probeX + b.w && num2 * TILE + TILE > probeX)) return;
  // 抬升（:3750-3770）：半砖上一格 → rowTop-8；本格半砖 → rowTop+8；上限 16.1px
  let target = num3 * TILE;
  if (halfAt(num2, num3 - 1)) target -= 8;
  else if (halfAt(num2, num3)) target += 8;
  if (target >= b.y + b.h) return;
  if (b.y + b.h - target > 16.1) return;
  b.y = target - b.h;
}

/** 格子的半砖碰撞盒（原版 Collision.cs:1320-1324 三处一致）：下半 8px。
 *  返回 null = 非实心；[top, bottom] = 碰撞盒的像素 y 区间 */
function solidSpan(world: World, tx: number, ty: number): [number, number] | null {
  const st = world.store;
  if (!st.isSolid(tx, ty)) return null;
  const top = ty * TILE + (st.half[st.idx(tx, ty)] ? 8 : 0);
  return [top, (ty + 1) * TILE];
}

/** Collision.StepDown（Collision.cs:3577-3638，gravDir=1、waterWalk=false）：
 *  贴地行走时脚下 7~17px 内有落面（半砖顶/台阶/平台）→ 直接吸附下去，
 *  消除下楼梯的腾空帧（onGround 连续，AI 跳跃/攻击门不抖动）。
 *  玩家（Player.cs:23252，vy==gravity 时）与 NPC（NPC.cs:54377，vy==0 时）共用。 */
function applyStepDown(b: Body, world: World): void {
  const vx = b.vx;
  if (vx === 0) return;
  const st = world.store;
  const probeX = b.x + vx;                          // vector.X = position.X + velocity.X
  // vector.Y = ⌊(y+h)/16⌋×16 - h（:3581 先把脚底吸附到格线）
  const snapY = Math.floor((b.y + b.h) / TILE) * TILE - b.h;
  const rowA = Math.floor((snapY + b.h + 4) / TILE); // num3（:3585）
  const col0 = Math.floor(probeX / TILE), col1 = Math.floor((probeX + b.w) / TILE);
  const num4 = Math.floor(b.h / TILE) + (b.h % TILE !== 0 ? 1 : 0);
  let best = (rowA + num4) * TILE;                  // num5 初始（远下方默认）
  for (let i = col0; i <= col1; i++) {
    for (let j = rowA; j <= rowA + 1; j++) {
      if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) continue;
      const ji = st.idx(i, j);
      if (!(st.isSolid(i, j) || st.isPlatform(i, j))) continue; // tileSolid||tileSolidTop（:3614）
      let top = j * TILE;
      if (st.half[ji]) top += 8;                    // 半砖顶 +8（:3618-3621）
      // FloatIntersect(tile 行, 以原 position 判定 :3623)
      if (i * TILE < b.x + b.w && i * TILE + TILE > b.x
        && j * TILE - 17 < b.y + b.h && j * TILE - 17 + TILE > b.y && top < best) {
        best = top;
      }
    }
  }
  const gap = best - (b.y + b.h);                   // num10（:3630）
  if (gap > 7 && gap < 17) b.y = best - b.h;        // :3632-3637
}

/** 单个轴的移动 + 碰撞解析。返回是否发生碰撞。 */

````
Now I understand the architecture. Key gap: **no waterMovementSpeed-style slowdown at movement integration**. In vanilla, `Collision_MoveWhileWet` moves position by `velocity * Slowdown` (0.5 water, 0.5 lava, 0.25 honey, 0.375 shimmer) — applied for ALL NPCs when wet (except those in the never-wet list, which then take Collision_MoveWhileDry).

The repo instead has ad-hoc buoyancy for slimes (`buoyant`) and no general wet slowdown. So the user's observed bug ("monsters attack normally underwater, no slowdown") matches: vanilla halves their positional movement while wet.

Now the plan:

1. **Wet detection 1:1**: Implement vanilla `WetCollision` (centered box: 10×height/2 at center) — the repo's `liquid[center tile] > 80` is a crude approximation. Implement `wet`/`honeyWet`/`shimmerWet`/`lavaWet`/`wetCount` properly. Also the never-wet exemption list: `(type==690 && ai[0]==0) || type==72 || aiStyle==21 || aiStyle==67 || type==376 || type==579 || type==541 || (aiStyle==7 && ai[0]==25)`.

2. **Movement integration**: apply `position += velocity * factor` while wet. In our engine, moveAndCollide(dx, dy) takes explicit dx/dy, so the natural hook: scale the dx/dy passed to moveAndCollide by the factor. But there are ~40 call sites of moveAndCollide across Enemy.ts... That's the conflict concern. Better: implement the scaling INSIDE moveAndCollide via a Body field, e.g. `b.liquidMoveFactor` (default 1), and set it once per tick in Enemy.fixedUpdate based on wet state. That's a minimal hook: one field on Body + 2 lines in moveAndCollide + computation in Enemy.fixedUpdate. 

Wait — but vanilla semantics: `position += velocity * Slowdown`, and `velocity` itself is unchanged. Our moveAndCollide(b, world, b.vx, b.vy) integrates dx=b.vx, dy=b.vy. So scaling dx,dy by factor = exactly vanilla semantics, and vx/vy remain unchanged (they do, since moveAndCollide doesn't modify velocity except... let me check — moveAxis may modify velocity? Let me check whether moveAndCollide zeroes velocity on collision. Looking at the loop: `if (hitX) { b.hitWall = true; dx = 0; }` — it zeroes local dx, not b.vx. Let me check moveAxis to see if it modifies b.vx/b.vy.

Also vanilla has the collideX/collideY nuance: if tile collision changed velocity (velocity != oldDryVelocity), then use unscaled velocity on that axis. In vanilla, ApplyTileCollision modifies velocity on collision (velocity.X = 0 etc.). In our moveAndCollide, b.vx/b.vy are NOT modified by collision (the Entity keeps its velocity; AI applies friction). Now I have enough vanilla understanding. Let me now design the implementation. Priorities per the user's request:

### P0 — the reported bug: no wet slowdown
Vanilla: every wet NPC's positional movement is scaled by waterMovementSpeed (0.5) / lava 0.5 / honey 0.25 / shimmer 0.375 at the single integration point. Implement as a per-tick `liquidMoveFactor` on the Body consumed by moveAndCollide. Hook: compute in Enemy.fixedUpdate once (1:1 UpdateCollision), and pass through. Also player? No — player has its own liquid handling in Player.ts (out of scope, but let me check if Player already has it — the task is enemy-focused; the user said "怪物能下水照常攻击").

### P0 — wet detection 1:1
Implement vanilla `WetCollision` (centered 10×h/2 box + liquid surface height adjustment + slope case) rather than `liquid[center] > 80`. This matters: with liquid > 0 on center tile but low liquid, vanilla may not be wet. Also for shallow water on slopes.

Never-wet exemption list: `(type==690&&ai[0]==0) || type==72 || aiStyle==21 || aiStyle==67 || type==376 || type==579 || type==541 || (aiStyle==7&&ai[0]==25)` — these NPCs never get wet (no slowdown, no lava via water path). Type 72 = Blazing Wheel, 376 = Sleeping Angler, 579 = Drunk bartender... wait, but repo's boundNPCAI floats 376/579 on water using wet! Let me check vanilla AI_000: at 19818... The user's prompt says "376 睡渔夫/579 醉酒酒保水上漂流（wet → vy=-0.4 上浮 + 缓漂）" — from repo comment citing cs:19818-19883. But Collision_WaterCollision exempts 376/579 from wet detection! Let me verify AI_000 around 19774-19893 to see how they detect water.

//   TouchDamageHot {37,58,76,684,230}           —— 站上灼烧 Burning(0.33s 刷新)
//   TouchDamageBleeding {48,232,750}            —— 尖刺族,流血(停自然恢复)
//   TouchDamageImmediate {32:10,69:17,80:6,352:10,655:100,48:60,232:80,484:25,750:70}
// CanTileHurt 门禁:230 仅 ftw 种子 / 80(仙人掌) 仅饥荒种子 → 普通世界恒无效。
import { TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
import type { TileStore } from '../world/TileStore';

// sheet id → 本仓库 internal tile id（取首个 v_ 定义）
const BY_SHEET = new Map<number, number>();
for (let id = 1; id < TILE_DEFS.length; id++) {
  const s = TILE_DEFS[id]?.vanilla?.sheet;
  if (s !== undefined && !BY_SHEET.has(s)) BY_SHEET.set(s, id);
}
const tid = (sheet: number): number => BY_SHEET.get(sheet) ?? -1;
const setOf = (sheets: number[]) => new Set(sheets.map(tid).filter((v) => v >= 0));
src/data/npcLavaImmune.ts
src/world/TileStore.ts

/** 单个轴的移动 + 碰撞解析。返回是否发生碰撞。 */
function moveAxis(b: Body, world: World, dx: number, dy: number): { hitX: boolean; hitY: boolean } {
  let hitX = false, hitY = false;
  const st = world.store;
  const avx = Math.abs(b.vx);
  // 原版坡面放行门（Collision.cs:2361-2387 flag3）：从高/低侧贴面走近的坡面格
  // 不参与本轴碰撞（交给 slopeCollide 对角线贴合）。全部以【移动前】位置判定
  // （原版 vector3,:2306）——传参 ox/oy,勿用移动后的 b.x/b.y。
  // 地面坡门是 feet-|vx| <= 格底(top+16,:2375/:2379 的 num7=格高)——不是格顶!
  // 写成格顶会让低侧贴地进入永远不过 → 孤立坡前一格被 X 拦截卡死。
  // slope1 左高右低 / 2 右高左低 / 3 左低右高(天花板) / 4 右低左高(天花板)
  const slopePass = (tx: number, ty: number, ox: number, oy: number): boolean => {
    const j = st.idx(tx, ty);
    const sl = st.slope[j];
    if (sl === 0 || st.half[j]) return false;
    const top = ty * TILE;
    if (sl === 1) return oy + b.h - avx <= top + TILE && ox >= tx * TILE;
    if (sl === 2) return oy + b.h - avx <= top + TILE && ox + b.w <= tx * TILE + TILE;
    if (sl === 3) return oy + avx >= top && ox >= tx * TILE;
    return oy + avx >= top && ox + b.w <= tx * TILE + TILE; // 4
  };
  // X 轴
  if (dx !== 0) {
    b.x += dx;
    const dir = Math.sign(dx);
    const oldX = b.x - dx; // 本步移动前位置（原版 vector3）
    const edgeX = dir > 0 ? b.x + b.w : b.x;
    const tx = Math.floor(edgeX / TILE);
    const y0 = Math.floor(b.y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((b.y + b.h - 0.01) / TILE);
    for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {
      if (slopePass(tx, ty, oldX, b.y)) continue;
      const span = solidSpan(world, tx, ty);
      // 半砖只占下半：身体底部没超过半砖顶面（ty*16+8）则不拦
      if (span && b.y + b.h > span[0]) {
        // 原版拦截前提（:2406/:2426）：上一位置【完全】在该格一侧（贴面接近）才拦；
        // 身体已横向跨在格上（上坡爬升中段,脚沉在斜面下）不拦——交给 slopeCollide
        // 抬升,否则上坡会被每帧推回卡死
        if (dir > 0 ? oldX + b.w > tx * TILE + 0.01 : oldX < (tx + 1) * TILE - 0.01) continue;
        // hoik 坡链放行（:2412/:2432）：身后格是配套坡面（同向坡链中段）→ 不拦
        const bj = st.idx(tx - Math.sign(dir), ty);
        if (dir > 0 && (st.slope[bj] === 2 || st.slope[bj] === 4)) continue;
        if (dir < 0 && (st.slope[bj] === 1 || st.slope[bj] === 3)) continue;
        if (dir > 0) b.x = tx * TILE - b.w;
        else b.x = (tx + 1) * TILE;
        b.vx = 0;
        hitX = true;
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  // Y 轴
  if (dy !== 0) {
    const oldY = b.y; // 移动前位置（原版 vector3.Y）
    b.y += dy;
    const dir = Math.sign(dy);
    const edgeY = dir > 0 ? b.y + b.h : b.y;
    const ty = Math.floor(edgeY / TILE);
    const x0 = Math.floor(b.x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((b.x + b.w - 0.01) / TILE);
    // 原版取整行最高面（Collision.cs:1610-1631 num13 取最大盒顶），不能 break 在最左列——
    // 否则左列半砖/右列整砖时身体会嵌进整砖 8px
    let bestTop = Infinity;
    for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
      const solid = world.store.isSolid(tx, ty);
      const span = solidSpan(world, tx, ty);
      const plat = !b.dropThrough && dir > 0 && world.store.isPlatform(tx, ty)
        && (b.y + b.h) - dy <= ty * TILE + 1; // 上一位置在平台之上
      if (dir > 0) {
        if (!solid && !plat) continue;
        if (slopePass(tx, ty, b.x, oldY)) continue; // 坡面格交给 slopeCollide 对角线贴合
        // 落地门槛（Collision.cs:1610/1631）：新底部越过盒顶 且 上一位置在盒顶之上，
        // 否则（嵌入/侧入）不吸附——半砖盒顶是 ty*16+8，只进入行上半不算落地
        const top = span ? span[0] : ty * TILE;
        if (b.y + b.h <= top || (b.y + b.h) - dy > top + 0.01) continue;
        if (top < bestTop) bestTop = top;
      } else {
        if (!solid) continue;
        if (slopePass(tx, ty, b.x, oldY)) continue;
        // 上顶：上一位置在盒底之下才命中；取最低盒底（最先撞到的天花板）
        const bottom = (ty + 1) * TILE;
        if (b.y >= bottom || b.y - dy < bottom - 0.01) continue;
6:export const GRAVITY = 0.4; // px/tick^2
7:export const MAX_FALL_SPEED = 10;
12:// 掉落物独立物理（WorldItem.cs:418-450：空气 0.1/7、水 0.08/5、蜂蜜 0.05/3）——勿与玩家 GRAVITY 耦合
13:export const ITEM_GRAVITY = 0.1;
17:export const ENEMY_GRAVITY = 0.32;

```

moveAxis DOES zero `b.vx` on X hit (`b.vx = 0`) — so it DOES modify velocity! Let me check the Y axis too — it likely zeroes b.vy on Y hit. So the repo's moveAndCollide zeroes velocity on collision (unlike vanilla where velocity is preserved and AI applies friction). That matters for the liqFactor semantics:

In vanilla Collision_MoveWhileWet:
```
vector = velocity * Slowdown;
if (velocity.X != oldDryVelocity.X) { vector.X = velocity.X; collideX = true; }
```
The check `velocity != oldDryVelocity` detects that ApplyTileCollision modified velocity (on collision, vanilla zeroes velocity on that axis). If modified → use the (already zeroed) actual velocity, i.e., no slowdown on that axis, and set collideX.

In our engine, moveAndCollide zeroes b.vx/b.vy on collision DURING the movement — which happens after scaling. Scaling dx by 0.5 means the enemy moves half as far into a wall; collision still triggers. Roughly equivalent. Fine.

Note GRAVITY = 0.4, MAX_FALL_SPEED = 10. Vanilla fish out-of-water gravity is 0.3 with clamp 10. The repo uses GRAVITY(0.4). For 1:1, aiStyle16 out-of-water uses 0.3; jellyfish uses 0.2. There's ENEMY_GRAVITY = 0.32 constant. Hmm — which is used where? Enemy uses GRAVITY (0.4). Vanilla NPC gravity default is... NPC.gravity default 0.3 (used in Collision_WalkDownSlopes & ApplyTileCollision). But the repo standardized on GRAVITY=0.4 for enemies (which matches... hmm, vanilla NPC gravity = 0.3f. The repo chose 0.4 — a global deviation, not mine to fix today).

For the out-of-water branches, vanilla explicitly writes `velocity.Y += 0.3f; clamp 10`. I'll use the literal 0.3 for these branches (1:1), noting the repo's GRAVITY=0.4 is a separate global deviation.

 "old_string": "  /** AI_018 水母族（NPC.145.cs:24210+）：阻尼漂移 + 周期性脉冲游向目标\n   *  速度 *=0.98 阻尼（L24349）；静止阈值内（num277 0.2-3）触发脉冲，脉冲速度 7-9（L24374+）。\n   *  离水：重力下坠拍打（脉冲只在水中生效——否则水母会飞） */\n  private jellyfishAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {\n    const st = game.world.store;\n    const inWater = st.liquid[st.idx(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE))] > 80;\n    this.vx *= 0.98; this.vy *= 0.98;\n    if (inWater) {\n      if (++this.aiT % 90 === 0 && player) {\n        const dx = player.cx - this.cx, dy = player.cy - this.cy;\n        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n        this.vx = (dx / d) * 7;       // 脉冲速度 7（大体型 9）\n        this.vy = (dy / d) * 7;\n      } else if (!player) {\n        this.vy -= 0.02;              // 缓慢下沉漂移\n      }\n    } else {\n      this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED); // 离水受重力\n      if (this.onGround && this.aiT % 30 === 0) this.vy = -2.5; // 拍打\n    }\n    moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);\n    if (this.hitWall) this.vx *= -0.8;\n    if (this.onGround && inWater) this.vy = -Math.abs(this.vy) - 0.5;\n  }",
 "new_string": "  /** AI_018 水母族（NPC.145.cs:24210+）：阻尼漂移 + 周期性脉冲游向目标\n   *  速度 *=0.98 阻尼（L24349）；静止阈值内（num277 0.2-3）触发脉冲，脉冲速度 7-9（L24374+）。\n   *  离水（cs:24459-24474）：rotation+=vx×0.1、vy==0 时 vx ×0.98 阻尼、重力 0.2 钳 10\n   *  ——水母离水只坠不蹦（与 aiStyle16 鱼的拍打挣扎不同） */\n  private jellyfishAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {\n    const inWater = this.wet;\n    this.vx *= 0.98; this.vy *= 0.98;\n    if (inWater) {\n      if (++this.aiT % 90 === 0 && player) {\n        const dx = player.cx - this.cx, dy = player.cy - this.cy;\n        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n        this.vx = (dx / d) * 7;       // 脉冲速度 7（大体型 9）\n        this.vy = (dy / d) * 7;\n      } else if (!player) {\n        this.vy -= 0.02;              // 缓慢下沉漂移\n      }\n    } else if (this.vy === 0) {\n      this.vx *= 0.98;                // 离水阻尼（cs:24463-24467）\n      if (Math.abs(this.vx) < 0.01) this.vx = 0;\n    }\n    if (!inWater) this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + 0.2, 10); // 离水重力 0.2 钳 10（cs:24469）\n    moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);\n    if (this.hitWall) this.vx *= -0.8;\n    if (this.onGround && inWater) this.vy = -Math.abs(this.vy) - 0.5;\n  }"

/** 原版 Collision.SlopeCollision（1456 Collision.cs:1796-2036）适配：
 *  常规碰撞后对重叠坡面格做对角线贴合——身体沉到斜面下时抬回斜面上
 *  （走路沿坡爬升;多格取最高贴合位,与原版 y 最小值机制一致）。
 *  slope1/2 地面坡:slope1 左高右低(左行爬升)、slope2 右高左低;
 *  slope3/4 天花板坡:对称向下推离。
 *  受阻回退已对齐原版 :2004-2033（速度清零 + slope 向性 X 补偿）。
 *  与原版的偏差（稳定性取舍,均在行内注释标记）：
 *  - num4 钳 ≥0（原版 num4<0 跳过）：坡顶过渡瞬间防脱钩下沉
 *  - 未移植 :2003 的"重跑 TileCollision 校验"本体（分轴结构代价高,
 *    钳位已覆盖其主收益;受阻回退语义由嵌入守卫分支等价提供） */
function slopeCollide(b: Body, world: World): void {
  const st = world.store;
  const x0 = Math.floor(b.x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((b.x + b.w - 0.01) / TILE);
  const y0 = Math.floor(b.y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((b.y + b.h - 0.01) / TILE);
  let bestLift = 0;      // 最大抬升量（负 y 位移;0 = 无贴合）
  let bestDrop = 0;      // 天花板最大下推量
  for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
    for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {
      if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;
      const i = st.idx(tx, ty);
      const slope = st.slope[i];
      if (slope === 0 || st.half[i] || !st.isSolid(tx, ty)) continue;
      const vx0 = tx * TILE, vy0 = ty * TILE;
      if (b.x + b.w <= vx0 || b.x >= vx0 + TILE || b.y + b.h <= vy0 || b.y >= vy0 + TILE) continue;
      // 对角线水平偏移（:1882-1893/1927-1934）：slope1/3 取身体左缘进格深度,
      // slope2/4 取右缘。钳到 ≥0（与原版 num4<0 即跳过的差异,见函数头注释）：
      // 坡顶过渡瞬间(高侧缘越过坡面)原版会脱钩,脚底残留在对角线端点下方数像素,

//  4. 掉落物入微光端到端：进度累积 → 转化换物品（原堆叠保留）+ 钱币消失入 luck
//  5. 玩家脱困落点搜索（findShimmerFreeSpot：微光内不放、最近空气落点优先）

describe('转化表提取（ItemID.cs / NPCID.cs Sets）', () => {
  it('物品对 312 条 + 抽查已知转化（含火炬 8 → 5353 双向链）', () => {
    expect(getTransformToItem(3460)).toBe(947);      // 夜明锭链头
    expect(getTransformToItem(947)).toBe(1106);
    expect(getTransformToItem(8)).toBe(5353);        // 火炬 → 骨炬
    expect(getTransformToItem(206)).toBe(207);       // 凝胶 ↔ 精炼凝胶族
    expect(getTransformToItem(3461)).toBe(0);        // 无月相参 → 动态分支不启用（表外）
    // 月相砖动态分支全 8 相位（ShimmerTransforms.cs:108-125;MoonPhase 枚举序 0-7）
    const LUNAR = [5408, 5401, 5403, 5402, 5406, 5407, 5405, 5404]; // Full/TQL/HL/QL/Empty/QR/HR/TQR
    for (let ph = 0; ph < 8; ph++) {
      expect(getTransformToItem(3461, ph)).toBe(LUNAR[ph]);
      // 转化目标物品必须已注册(★曾缺 5402/5406/5408 → 对应月相转化静默丢物)
      expect(internalIdOfVanilla(LUNAR[ph])).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
    }
    expect(canShimmerItem(3461, false, 3)).toBe(true); // 有月相 → 可微光化(无参时 false)
    expect(getTransformToItem(4837)).toBe(999);      // 绿宝石 → 金皇冠（源在键侧）
  });

    expect(canShimmerItem(1326, true)).toBe(true);
  });

    expect(isNpcTownShimmerable(17)).toBe(true);       // 商人
    expect(isNpcTownShimmerable(1)).toBe(false);       // 蓝史莱姆非城镇
  });
});

export interface VanillaNpc {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  frames: number;
  lifeMax: number;
  damage: number;
  defense: number;
  knockBackResist: number;
  aiStyle: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  npcSlots: number;
  noGravity: boolean;
  noTileCollide: boolean;
  friendly: boolean;
  townNPC: boolean;
  HitSound: string;   // SoundID 名（NPCHitN / NPCDeathN）
  DeathSound: string;
  scale: number;
  /** SetDefaults alpha：每类型静态不透明度基线（渲染 1-alpha/255，NPC.Opacity；无通用渐隐） */
  alpha?: number;
  /** SetDefaults color：非 default 时 Main.DrawNPC 二次绘制同贴图（GetColor 逐像素乘法贴轮廓） */
  color?: number[];  // [r, g, b, a]
  critter?: boolean;  // NPCID.Sets.CountsAsCritter 小动物（tools/extract-critters.mjs 提取）
}

export const VANILLA_NPCS = vanillaNpcsJson as unknown as Record<string, VanillaNpc>;

/** GetGemSquirrelToSpawn（NPC.cs:5611-5623）/ GetGemBunnyToSpawn（:5591-5603）：
 *  纯加权随机表（Diamond 5%/Amber 8%/Ruby 10%/Emerald 12%/Sapphire 16%/Topaz 21%/Amethyst 28%）
 *  ——与就近宝石 tile、世界档位完全无关 */
export function getGemSquirrelToSpawn(rand: () => number): number {
  const num = Math.floor(rand() * 100);
  if (num < 5) return 644;
  if (num < 13) return 645;
  if (num < 23) return 643;

Full slime wet branch (1:1):
```
if (wet) {
  if (collideY) vy = -2;
  if (vy < 0 && ai[3] == position.X) { direction *= -1; ai[2] = 200; }
  if (vy > 0) ai[3] = position.X;
  if (type == 59 && !remixWorld) {           // Lava Slime
    if (vy > 2) vy *= 0.9;
    else if (directionY < 0) vy -= 0.8;
    vy -= 0.5;
    if (vy < -10) vy = -10;
  } else {
    if (vy > 2) vy *= 0.9;
    vy -= 0.5;
    if (vy < -4) vy = -4;
  }
  if (ai[2] == 1 && flag3) TargetClosest();
}
```
So slimes DO have buoyancy in vanilla: vy>2 → ×0.9; vy -= 0.5; clamp -4. That's a constant upward push capped at -4 — the slime bobs near the surface. The repo's buoyant hack (vy -= 0.32, vy *= 0.92, vx *= 0.95) was a rough approximation of THIS. Now I can implement it 1:1 in slimeAI and remove the buoyant hack.

Note where it sits in vanilla AI_001: AFTER the main hop logic, near the end (before aiAction=0 section). The slime AI continues with jump logic after. In the repo, slimeAI calls groundPhysics at the end (via `this.groundPhysics(game, 0, 0.12, 1, true)` inside... let me look at the full slimeAI again to place the wet branch correctly.

Also note `ai[3]` is used here (the repo's ai3 field is used for other things — "地牢 AI 族用：诅咒头骨状态" per the comment at line 656-657. ai[3] in slime AI = position.X memory for the water turn logic). The repo slimeAI doesn't track ai3 for this. I'll add a dedicated field? There's `ai3` — I could reuse it but it's semantically overloaded per-AI; the comment says ai3 is "原版 ai[3]（地牢 AI 族用...）". Since AI families reuse ai[] slots for different purposes, using this.ai3 here is consistent with the existing pattern (ai0/ai1/ai2 are reused across families). I'll use this.ai3.

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T02:54:48.962Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

````
Now I'll implement the 1:1 slime wet branch. Placement in vanilla: after the attack sections (61877 comes after the queen minion block at 61840ish? Let me check order: 61668-61840 queen minions, then 61844-61876 dust, then 61877 wet, then aiAction section). The repo's order: hop logic → spiked → queenMinion → groundPhysics. So the wet branch goes right before groundPhysics.

The 1:1 branch needs `collideY` — in our engine, `collideY` ≈ hitHead || onGround (any Y-axis collision this tick). But careful: the branch reads the PREVIOUS tick's collideY (vanilla AI runs before UpdateCollision; collideY is from last tick's collision). Our slimeAI runs before moveAndCollide in the same tick, so `this.onGround || this.hitHead` reflects the last moveAndCollide — same semantics. 

Implementation:
```ts
// ---- wet 浮力段（cs:61877-61935，1:1 替换旧 groundPhysics buoyant 近似）----
// 纵向碰撞 → vy=-2 上弹；上浮中 X 没动 → 掉头 + ai[2]=200；vy>2 ×0.9、恒 -0.5 上浮、
// 钳 -4（59 岩浆史莱姆钳 -10 且 directionY<0 时再 -0.8）——史莱姆在水里上下弹跳漂浮
if (this.wet) {
  if (this.onGround || this.hitHead) this.vy = -2;
  if (this.vy < 0 && this.ai3 === Math.floor(this.x)) { this.facing *= -1; this.ai2 = 200; }
  if (this.vy > 0) this.ai3 = Math.floor(this.x);
  if (this.vanillaId === 59) {
    if (this.vy > 2) this.vy *= 0.9;
    else if (this.dirY < 0) this.vy -= 0.8;

'../src/entities/types';\n\nconst W = 60, H = 256;\n\n/** 水池 x∈[10,30) 行 33-39 满水、行 40 池底；x∈[30,46) 行 40 陆地 */\nfunction liquidWorld(liquidType = 1, depth = 255): World {\n  const w = new World(W, H, 11, 'npc-liquid-test');\n  const st = w.store;\n  for (let x = 10; x < 30; x++) {\n    st.setTile(x, 40, 1);\n    for (let y = 33; y < 40; y++) st.setLiquid(x, y, depth, liquidType);\n  }\n  for (let x = 30; x < 46; x++) st.setTile(x, 40, 1);\n  return w;\n}\n\nfunction hooks(w: World, p: Player): GameHooks {\n  return {\n    world: w, player: p,\n    enemies: () => [], critters: () => [],\n    spawnDrop: () => null,\n    damagePlayer: () => {},\n    addDamageNumber: () => {}, cutTile: () => {},\n    onEnemyKilled: () => {}, spawnEnemy: () => {},\n    spawnParticles: () => {}, notifyInventoryChanged: () => {},\n    playSfx: () => {}, playSfxFiles: () => {}, showPickupLabel: () => {},\n  } as unknown as GameHooks;\n}\n\nconst host = (over: Partial<Parameters<typeof liquidMoveFactor>[0]> = {}) =>\n  ({\n    x: 0, y: 0, w: 18, h: 40, vx: 0, vy: 0, cx: 0, cy: 0,\n    vanillaId: 3, vanilla: { aiStyle: 3 }, ai0: 0,\n    wet: false, wetCount: 0, honeyWet: false, shimmerWet: false, lavaWet: false,\n    ...over,\n  }) as Parameters<typeof liquidMoveFactor>[0];\n\ndescribe('Collision.WetCollision（Collision.cs:1575）中心小盒 + 液面收缩', () => {\n  const w = liquidWorld();\n  it('深水中心 → wet；水上空气 → 干', () => {\n    expect(wetCollision(w.store, 20 * TILE, 36 * TILE, 18, 40).wet).toBe(true);\n    expect(wetCollision(w.store, 20 * TILE, 20 * TILE, 18, 40).wet).toBe(false);\n  });\n  it('半格浅水（liquid=128）：液面下 8px 有效——上半不入水、下半入水', () => {\n    const s = liquidWorld(1, 128);\n    // 盒中心在浅水格上半（y=34*16+2）→ 盒底 34*16+22 仍高于液面 34*16+8+… 需精确算：\n    // drop=(256-128)/32=4 → surfY=行顶+8、有效高 8。中心 34*16+2、盒高 h/2=8 → 盒区间\n    // [34*16-2, 34*16+6) 全在液面(34*16+8)之上 → 干\n    expect(wetCollision(s.store, 20 * TILE, 34 * TILE - 6, 18, 16).wet).toBe(false);\n    // 盒沉到液面之下 → wet\n    expect(wetCollision(s.store, 20 * TILE, 34 * TILE + 2, 18, 16).wet).toBe(true);\n  });\n  it('蜂蜜/微光格带类型标记（选减速档用）', () => {\n    const h = liquidWorld(3);\n    expect(wetCollision(h.store, 20 * TILE, 36 * TILE, 18, 40)).toMatchObject({ wet: true, honey: true });\n    const sh = liquidWorld(4);\n    expect(wetCollision(sh.store, 20 * TILE, 36 * TILE, 18, 40)).toMatchObject({ wet: true, shimmer: true });\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('减速因子表（NPC.cs:5946-5952 + :93748-93762 优先级）', () => {\n  it('默认 水 .5 / 岩浆 .5 / 蜂蜜 .25 / 微光 .375；干 = 1', () => {\n    expect(liquidMoveFactor(host({ wet: true }))).toBeCloseTo(0.5, 5);\n    expect(liquidMoveFactor(host({ wet: true, lavaWet: true }))).toBeCloseTo(0.5, 5);\n    expect(liquidMoveFactor(host({ wet: true, honeyWet: true }))).toBeCloseTo(0.25, 5);\n    expect(liquidMoveFactor(host({ wet: true, shimmerWet: true }))).toBeCloseTo(0.375, 5);\n    // 优先级：微光 > 蜂蜜 > 岩浆\n    expect(liquidMoveFactor(host({ wet: true, shimmerWet: true, honeyWet: true }))).toBeCloseTo(0.375, 5);\n    expect(liquidMoveFactor(host())).toBe(1);\n  });\n  it('DD2 族（548/549/551-578/620）与水黾 612/613 液体全速', () => {\n    for (const id of [548, 549, 551, 560, 578, 620]) {\n      expect(liquidMoveFactor(host({ vanillaId: id, wet: true })), `npc ${id}`).toBe(1);\n    }\n    expect(liquidMoveFactor(host({ vanillaId: 612, wet: true }))).toBe(1);\n    expect(liquidMoveFactor(host({ vanillaId: 613, wet: true }))).toBe(1);\n    expect(liquidMoveFactor(host({ vanillaId: 550, wet: true }))).toBeCloseTo(0.5, 5); // 表外\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('永不沾水族（NPC.cs:94252）', () => {\n  it('72 火轮/376 睡渔夫/579 醉酒酒保/541 沙尘精/aiStyle21/67/(690,ai0=0)/(7,ai0=25)', () => {\n    expect(skipsWet(host({ vanillaId: 72, vanilla: { aiStyle: 20 } }))).toBe(true);\n    expect(skipsWet(host({ vanillaId: 376 }))).toBe(true);\n    expect(skipsWet(host({ vanillaId: 579 }))).toBe(true);\n    expect(skipsWet(host({ vanillaId: 541 }))).toBe(true);\n    expect(skipsWet(host({ vanillaId: 158, vanilla: { aiStyle: 21 } }))).toBe(true);\n    expect(skipsWet(host({ vanillaId: 100, vanilla: { aiStyle: 67 } }))).toBe(true);\n    expect(skipsWet(host({ vanillaId: 690, ai0: 0 }))).toBe(true);\n    expect(skipsWet(host({ vanillaId: 441, vanilla: { aiStyle: 7 }, ai0: 25 }))).toBe(true);\n    // 反例\n    expect(skipsWet(host({ vanillaId: 3 }))).toBe(false);\n    expect(skipsWet(host({ vanillaId: 690, ai0: 1 }))).toBe(false);\n    expect(skipsWet(host({ vanillaId: 441, vanilla: { aiStyle: 7 }, ai0: 0 }))).toBe(false);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('Collision_MoveWhileWet（:93986）：位移 ×factor、velocity 不变', () => {\n  it('liqFactor .5：vx=2 只走 1px，b.vx 仍是 2', () => {\n    const w = liquidWorld();\n    const b = {\n      x: 31 * TILE, y: 38 * TILE, w: 18, h: 24, vx: 2, vy: 0,\n      onGround: false, hitWall: false, hitHead: false, liqFactor: 0.5,\n    };\n    moveAndCollide(b, w, b.vx, b.vy);\n    expect(b.x).toBeCloseTo(31 * TILE + 1, 5);\n    expect(b.vx).toBe(2);\n  });\n  it('liqFactor 1（干/永不沾水族）：全速', () => {\n    const w = liquidWorld();\n    const b = {\n      x: 31 * TILE, y: 38 * TILE, w: 18, h: 24, vx: 2, vy: 0,\n      onGround: false, hitWall: false, hitHead: false, liqFactor: 1,\n    };\n    moveAndCollide(b, w, b.vx, b.vy);\n    expect(b.x).toBeCloseTo(31 * TILE + 2, 5);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('E2E：僵尸（NPC 3）入水/岩浆/熄火', () => {\n  it('入水 → wet 且 liqFactor=.5（水里该减速的主诉）', () => {\n    const w = liquidWorld();\n    const p = new Player(45 * TILE, 30 * TILE, new Inventory());\n    const h = hooks(w, p);\n    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(3, 20 * TILE, 36 * TILE)!;\n    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, h as never);\n    expect(e.wet).toBe(true);\n    expect(e.inWater).toBe(true);\n    expect(e.liqFactor).toBeCloseTo(0.5, 5);\n  });\n  it('岸上 → 干、liqFactor=1', () => {\n    const w = liquidWorld();\n    const p = new Player(45 * TILE, 30 * TILE, new Inventory());\n    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(3, 38 * TILE, 38 * TILE)!;\n    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks(w, p) as never);\n    expect(e.wet).toBe(false);\n    expect(e.liqFactor).toBe(1);\n  });\n  it('岩浆：30t 冷却内 50 直伤 + onFire 7s；lavaImmune 族（24）免伤', () => {\n    const w = liquidWorld(2);\n    const p = new Player(45 * TILE, 30 * TILE, new Inventory());\n    const h = hooks(w, p);\n    const z = Enemy.fromVanilla(3, 20 * TILE, 36 * TILE)!;\n    const hp0 = z.hp;\n    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) z.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, h as never);   // 触发一次 50 伤\n    expect(z.hp).toBeLessThan(hp0);\n    expect(z.onFireT).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(z.lavaWet).toBe(true);\n    const pixie = Enemy.fromVanilla(24, 20 * TILE, 36 * TILE)!;      // NPC_LAVA_IMMUNE 成员\n    const php0 = pixie.hp;\n    for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) pixie.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, h as never);\n    expect(pixie.hp).toBe(php0);                                     // 免伤\n    expect(pixie.lavaWet).toBe(true);                                // 但 lavaWet 仍置位（:94524）\n  });\n  it('入水熄火：onFire(24) 与 onFire3(323) 双清（TryRemovingWaterPerishableEffects）', () => {\n    const w = liquidWorld();\n    const p = new Player(45 * TILE, 30 * TILE, new Inventory());\n    const h = hooks(w, p);\n    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(3, 20 * TILE, 36 * TILE)!;\n    e.onFireT = 5; e.onFire3T = 5;\n    e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, h as never);\n    expect(e.onFireT).toBe(0);\n    expect(e.onFire3T).toBe(0);\n  });\n  it('永不沾水族入水不减速（72 火轮语义位）', () => {\n    const w = liquidWorld();\n    const p = new Player(45 * TILE, 30 * TILE, new Inventory());\n    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(541, 20 * TILE, 36 * TILE)!;   // 沙尘精（:94252 表内）\n    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks(w, p) as never);\n    expect(e.wet).toBe(false);\n    expect(e.liqFactor).toBe(1);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('水栖离水分支（aiStyle 16 :24019-24034）与史莱姆 wet 段（:61877）', () => {\n  it('食人鱼 58 搁浅：拍打挣扎（vy 出现负脉冲）且不飞天', () => {\n    const w = liquidWorld();\n    const p = new Player(45 * TILE, 30 * TILE, new Inventory());\n    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(58, 38 * TILE, 38 * TILE)!;    // 岸上实地\n    let minVy = 0;\n    for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) {\n      e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks(w, p) as never);\n      minVy = Math.min(minVy, e.vy);\n    }\n    expect(e.wet).toBe(false);\n    expect(minVy).toBeLessThan(-1);                             // rand(-5,-2) 拍打已出\n    expect(e.vy).toBeLessThanOrEqual(10);                       // 钳 10\n  });\n  it('鲨鱼 65 搁浅：只 ×0.94 阻尼不蹦（:24022-24026）', () => {\n    const w = liquidWorld();\n    const p = new Player(45 * TILE, 30 * TILE, new Inventory());\n    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(65, 38 * TILE, 38 * TILE)!;\n    e.vx = 2;\n    let flapped = false;\n    for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) {\n      const vyBefore = e.vy;\n      e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks(w, p) as never);\n      if (vyBefore === 0 && e.vy < -1) flapped = true;\n    }\n    expect(flapped).toBe(false);\n    expect(Math.abs(e.vx)).toBeLessThan(0.5);                   // ×0.94/t 衰减\n  });\n  it('史莱姆入水：wet 段 vy 恒 -0.5 上浮钳 -4（弹跳漂浮，非旧 buoyant 近似）', () => {\n    const w = liquidWorld();\n    const p = new e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks(w, p) as never);\n      if (e.vy < -0.5) sawRise = true;\n      expect(e.vy).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(-4.01);               // 钳 -4\n    }\n    expect(e.wet).toBe(true);\n    expect(sawRise).toBe(true);\n  });\n  it('睡渔夫 376 水面漂流不沉（AI_000 头顶格液体门 :19818）', () => {\n    const w = liquidWorld();\n    const p = new Player(45 * TILE, 30 * TILE, new Inventory());\n    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(376, 20 * TILE, 36 * TILE)!;\n    const y0 = e.y;\n    for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks(w, p) as never);\n    expect(e.y).toBeLessThanOrEqual(y0 + 2);
